ОБЩИЙ ПЛАН И ОТЧЁТ О РЕФАКТОРИНГЕ TELEGRAM-БОТА

1. Исправление багов в тестах и Pipeline компрессии (test_pipeline.py)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Проблема: Тест `test_run_compress_pipeline_returns_summary` падал из-за отсутствия вызова `save_summary`. В ходе пайплайна происходил выход с ошибкой, так как извлечение каузальных связей и доменов знаний падало из-за неправильно замокированных схем LLM (ошибка AttributeError: 'MockStructuredResult' object has no attribute ...). Кроме того, патчи использовались для старого `LegacyStorage`, а не для нового `MemoryStore`.
- Решение: 
  - Изменён способ сопоставления схем в `mock_oneshot_structured` со `str(schema)` на `schema.__name__`.
  - Исправлены имена проверяемых Pydantic-схем на актуальные: `CausalLinkExtractionResult` и `KnowledgeDomainsExtractionResult`.
  - Заменены моки (`patch("companion.storage.legacy.LegacyStorage.save_summary")`) на `patch.object(memory_store, "save_summary")` в тестах `test_pipeline.py`.
- Результат: Пайплайн компрессии успешно доходит до конца, моки отрабатывают корректно. Тесты `test_pipeline.py` теперь зелёные.

2. Безопасность мутирующих команд (Command Safety)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Проблема: LLM в фоне могла принимать решение на выполнение деструктивных/мутирующих команд (например, `add_todo`, `clear_done`, `delete_todo` и т.д.) без явного согласия пользователя, что могло привести к нежелательному изменению данных.
- Решение:
  - В `bot_core.py` заведен словарь `PENDING_COMMANDS` для отслеживания таких действий.
  - При обнаружении LLM интента на мутирующую команду (`command in MUTATING_COMMANDS`), если она была вызвана без явного слеша `/`, пользователю отправляется Inline-клавиатура с кнопками "✅ Выполнить" и "❌ Отмена" (каждый запрос получает уникальный `cmd_id`).
  - В `handlers/chat.py` добавлен хендлер `@dp.callback_query` (префиксы `cmd_ok:` и `cmd_no:`), который перехватывает клик, проверяет, что кликнул тот самый пользователь, и в случае подтверждения — маршрутизирует и выполняет команду.
- Результат: Повышена безопасность работы с памятью. Мутирующие действия из свободного текста теперь всегда требуют явного подтверждения.

3. Race Condition счётчика компрессий (Increment Meta)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Проблема: `increment_compress_count` в `MemoryStore` считывал значение и затем перезаписывал его. Из-за отсутствия атомарности в конкурентной асинхронной среде это могло приводить к потерям апдейтов счетчика.
- Решение:
  - В `storage/sqlite_db.py` добавлен метод `increment_meta(key, amount)`. 
  - Реализован атомарный инкремент на уровне SQLite с помощью инструкции `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT ... DO UPDATE SET value = CAST(...) RETURNING value`.
  - Метод `increment_compress_count` в `MemoryStore` переведён на использование нового атомарного SQL-запроса.
- Результат: Полностью исключены race conditions при увеличении мета-счётчиков базы данных.

4. Исправление тестов Retrieval и улучшение поиска (test_retrieval.py)
-------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Проблема: Тест `test_external_queries_prune_unrelated_facts` падал, так как факты отсеивались (семантический порог < 0.2 отфильтровывал все факты из-за отсутствия возвращаемых FAISS скоров в тестовой среде).
- Решение:
  - В тестовом вызове `retrieval_mgr.select()` добавлен явный проброс `faiss_scores`, имитирующий высокое совпадение для фактов с ключевым словом "погода". 
  - В самом слое Retrieval (`memory/retrieval.py`) ранее реализован строгий фильтр (`semantic < 0.2`) для явных команд на веб-поиск (`is_explicit_search_request`), который отсеивает личный мусор и оставляет только релевантную контексту поиска информацию.
- Результат: Поисковые запросы ("найди в гугле") теперь строго отбрасывают нерелевантные личные якорные факты. Тест успешен.

-### 1.3 ╨Я╨╛╨╗╨╜╨╛╤Б╤В╤М╤О ╨▓╤Л╤А╨╡╨╖╨░╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╡ slash-╨║╨╛╨╝╨░╨╜╨┤╤Л

-╨н╤В╨╕ ╨║╨╛╨╝╨░╨╜╨┤╤Л **╨╜╨╡ ╤Б╤Г╤Й╨╡╤Б╤В╨▓╤Г╤О╤В** ╨╜╨╕╨│╨┤╨╡ ╨▓ ╤В╨╡╨║╤Г╤Й╨╡╨╝ ╨║╨╛╨┤╨╡ тАФ ╨╜╨╕ ╨║╨░╨║ handlers, ╨╜╨╕ ╨║╨░╨║ ╨╕╨╜╤В╨╡╨╜╤В-╤А╨╛╤Г╤В╤Л:

-| `/yearbook` | ╨г╨┤╨░╨╗╨╡╨╜╨░ |

-| `/moodweek` | ╨г╨┤╨░╨╗╨╡╨╜╨░ (╨╜╨╡╤В ╨╜╨╕ handler, ╨╜╨╕ intent route) |

-## 2. ╨б╤В╨░╤В╤Г╤Б ╨╕╨╜╤В╨╡╨│╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╨╕ ╨║╨╛╨│╨╜╨╕╤В╨╕╨▓╨╜╤Л╤Е ╤Б╨╗╨╛╤С╨▓

-### 2.1 End-to-End Response Pipeline
-
-```
-╨б╨╛╨╛╨▒╤Й╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╡ тЖТ build_context()
-  тФЬтФАтФА analyze_message() [LLM]     тЖТ intent, mood, importance, user_state, command
-  тФЬтФАтФА log_message()                тЖТ SQLite + JSONL
-  тФЬтФАтФА auto_reasoning_context()     тЖТ goals, causal, predictions, world model
-  тФЬтФАтФА auto_add_event_from_message()тЖТ ╨░╨▓╤В╨╛-╤Б╨╛╨╖╨┤╨░╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╤Б╨╛╨▒╤Л╤В╨╕╨╣
-  тФЬтФАтФА _get_policy_decision()       тЖТ PolicyDecision ╨╕╨╖ analyzer state
-  тФФтФАтФА _load_retrieval_context()    тЖТ ctx_data dict ╤Б╨╛ ╨▓╤Б╨╡╨╝╨╕ ╨║╨╛╨│╨╜╨╕╤В╨╕╨▓╨╜╤Л╨╝╨╕ ╨┤╨░╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╝╨╕
-
-тЖТ _route_command() ╨╡╤Б╨╗╨╕ intent=command, confidence тЙе 0.92
-тЖТ handle_grounding() ╨╡╤Б╨╗╨╕ ╤П╨▓╨╜╤Л╨╣ ╨┐╨╛╨╕╤Б╨║╨╛╨▓╤Л╨╣ ╨╖╨░╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б
-тЖТ _generate_and_send_response()
-    тФЬтФАтФА retrieval_mgr.select()     тЖТ ContextBundle (╨▓╤Б╨╡ ╨║╨╛╨│╨╜╨╕╤В╨╕╨▓╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╤Д╨╕╤З╨╕)
-    тФЬтФАтФА bundle.to_prompt_block()   тЖТ ╤В╨╡╨║╤Б╤В ╨┤╨╗╤П LLM
-    тФЬтФАтФА _build_user_prompt_block() тЖТ causal/future reasoning hints
-    тФЬтФАтФА format_prompt_with_policy()тЖТ тХРтХРтХР RESPONSE POLICY тХРтХРтХР
-    тФЬтФАтФА chat.send_message()        тЖТ LLM call
-    тФЬтФАтФА enforce_constraints()      тЖТ ╨┐╨╛╤Б╤В-╨┐╤А╨╛╤Ж╨╡╤Б╤Б╨╕╨╜╨│ ╨╛╤В╨▓╨╡╤В╨░
-    тФЬтФАтФА run_self_critique()        тЖТ ╨╝╨╡╤В╨░-╨╛╤Ж╨╡╨╜╨║╨░ ╨╛╤В╨▓╨╡╤В╨░
-    тФЬтФАтФА apply_critique_to_text()   тЖТ ╤Е╨╡╨┤╨╢╨╕╤А╨╛╨▓╨░╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╨╜╨╡╤Г╨▓╨╡╤А╨╡╨╜╨╜╨╛╤Б╤В╨╕
-    тФЬтФАтФА should_retry_with_grounding() тЖТ fallback ╨╡╤Б╨╗╨╕ confidence < 0.55
-    тФФтФАтФА background_user_model_reflection() тЖТ ╤Д╨╛╨╜╨╛╨▓╨╛╨╡ ╨╛╨▒╨╜╨╛╨▓╨╗╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╡ user model
-```

-### 2.2 ╨з╤В╨╛ ╤А╨╡╨░╨╗╤М╨╜╨╛ ╨▓╨║╨╗╤О╤З╨╡╨╜╨╛ ╨▓ LLM-╨┐╤А╨╛╨╝╨┐╤В
-
-| ╨д╨╕╤З╨░ | ╨Т ╨┐╤А╨╛╨╝╨┐╤В╨╡? | ╨Ь╨╡╤Е╨░╨╜╨╕╨╖╨╝ |
-|---|---|---|
-| Active goals | тЬЕ ╨Ф╨Р | `[╨Р╨║╤В╨╕╨▓╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╤Ж╨╡╨╗╨╕]` ╤Б╨╡╨║╤Ж╨╕╤П ╨▓ `ContextBundle.to_prompt_block()` |
-| Causal links | тЬЕ ╨Ф╨Р | `[╨Я╤А╨╕╤З╨╕╨╜╨╜╨╛-╤Б╨╗╨╡╨┤╤Б╤В╨▓╨╡╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╣ ╨║╨╛╨╜╤В╨╡╨║╤Б╤В]` ╤Б╨╡╨║╤Ж╨╕╤П |
-| Predictions | тЬЕ ╨Ф╨Р | `[╨Я╤А╨╛╨│╨╜╨╛╨╖╤Л ╨╕ ╨╛╨╢╨╕╨┤╨░╨╜╨╕╤П]` ╤Б╨╡╨║╤Ж╨╕╤П |
-| World model | тЬЕ ╨Ф╨Р | `[╨Ь╨╛╨┤╨╡╨╗╤М ╨╝╨╕╤А╨░]` ╤Б╨╡╨║╤Ж╨╕╤П |
-| Facts | тЬЕ ╨Ф╨Р | `[╨а╨╡╨╗╨╡╨▓╨░╨╜╤В╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╤Д╨░╨║╤В╤Л]` ╤Б╨╡╨║╤Ж╨╕╤П |
-| Reflections | тЬЕ ╨Ф╨Р | `[╨Т╤Л╨▓╨╛╨┤╤Л ╨╛ ╨┐╨╛╨╗╤М╨╖╨╛╨▓╨░╤В╨╡╨╗╨╡]` ╤Б╨╡╨║╤Ж╨╕╤П |
-| Personality | тЬЕ ╨Ф╨Р | `[╨б╨╜╨╕╨╝╨╛╨║ ╨╗╨╕╤З╨╜╨╛╤Б╤В╨╕]` ╨▓ system instruction |
-| Master summary | тЬЕ ╨Ф╨Р | `[Master Summary]` ╨▓ system instruction |
-| Policy constraints | тЬЕ ╨Ф╨Р | `тХРтХРтХР RESPONSE POLICY тХРтХРтХР` ╨▓ ╨┐╤А╨╛╨╝╨┐╤В╨╡ + ╨┐╨╛╤Б╤В-╨┐╤А╨╛╤Ж╨╡╤Б╤Б╨╕╨╜╨│ |
-| Causal reasoning mode | тЬЕ ╨Ф╨Р | `[╨а╨╡╨╢╨╕╨╝ reasoning]` hint ╨┐╤А╨╕ `causal_trigger=True` |
-| Future reasoning mode | тЬЕ ╨Ф╨Р | `[╨а╨╡╨╢╨╕╨╝ reasoning]` hint ╨┐╤А╨╕ `future_trigger=True` |
-| Self-critique | тЪая╕П ╨Ъ╨Ю╨б╨Т╨Х╨Э╨Э╨Ю | ╨Э╨╡ ╨▓ ╨┐╤А╨╛╨╝╨┐╤В╨╡, ╨╜╨╛ ╨╝╨╛╨┤╨╕╤Д╨╕╤Ж╨╕╤А╤Г╨╡╤В ╨╛╤В╨▓╨╡╤В ╨┐╨╛╤Б╤В-╤Д╨░╨║╤В╤Г╨╝ |
-| User model (identity) | тЭМ ╨Э╨Х╨в | Write-only тАФ ╨┐╨╕╤И╨╡╤В╤Б╤П ╨▓ JSON, ╨╜╨╕╨║╨╛╨│╨┤╨░ ╨╜╨╡ ╤З╨╕╤В╨░╨╡╤В╤Б╤П ╨▓ ╨┐╤А╨╛╨╝╨┐╤В |

-## 3. ╨Т╤Л╤П╨▓╨╗╨╡╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╨┐╤А╨╛╨▒╨╗╨╡╨╝╤Л ╤В╨╡╨║╤Г╤Й╨╡╨│╨╛ ╨║╨╛╨┤╨░
-
-### 3.1 ЁЯФ┤ ╨Ъ╤А╨╕╤В╨╕╤З╨╡╤Б╨║╨╕╨╡
-
-#### 3.1.1 ╨г╤В╨╡╤З╨║╨░ ╤Д╨╛╨╜╨╛╨▓╤Л╤Е ╨╖╨░╨┤╨░╤З ╨▓ `main.py`
-
-**╨д╨░╨╣╨╗:** `companion/main.py:74`
-
-```python
-asyncio.create_task(proactive_ping_loop(bot))
-```
-
-╨Ч╨░╨┤╨░╤З╨░ `proactive_ping_loop` ╤Б╨╛╨╖╨┤╨░╤С╤В╤Б╤П fire-and-forget тАФ ╤Б╤Б╤Л╨╗╨║╨░ ╨╜╨░ task ╨╜╨╕╨│╨┤╨╡ ╨╜╨╡ ╤Б╨╛╤Е╤А╨░╨╜╤П╨╡╤В╤Б╤П. ╨Я╤А╨╕ ╨╖╨░╨▓╨╡╤А╤И╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╕ ╨▒╨╛╤В╨░ (`finally` ╨▒╨╗╨╛╨║ L78-81) ╨╖╨░╨┤╨░╤З╨░ **╨╜╨╡ ╨╛╤В╨╝╨╡╨╜╤П╨╡╤В╤Б╤П**. ╨в╨░╨║╨╢╨╡ `proactive_ping_loop` ╤Б╨╛╨┤╨╡╤А╨╢╨╕╤В ╨▒╨╡╤Б╨║╨╛╨╜╨╡╤З╨╜╤Л╨╣ `while True` ╤Б `asyncio.sleep(1800)` ╨╕ ╨╗╨╛╨▓╨╕╤В ╨▓╤Б╨╡ ╨╕╤Б╨║╨╗╤О╤З╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П, ╨┐╨╡╤А╨╡╨╖╨░╨┐╤Г╤Б╨║╨░╤П╤Б╤М тАФ ╨╖╨╛╨╝╨▒╨╕-╨╖╨░╨┤╨░╤З╨░.
-
-╨в╨░ ╨╢╨╡ ╨┐╤А╨╛╨▒╨╗╨╡╨╝╨░ ╨▓ `background_scheduler.safe_task()` (L36-54): ╨▓╤Б╨╡ `asyncio.create_task()` fire-and-forget ╨▒╨╡╨╖ ╤Е╤А╨░╨╜╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П ╤Б╤Б╤Л╨╗╨╛╨║. Python ╨╝╨╛╨╢╨╡╤В ╨╗╨╛╨│╨╕╤А╨╛╨▓╨░╤В╤М "Task exception was never retrieved" ╨╡╤Б╨╗╨╕ ╨╖╨░╨┤╨░╤З╨░ ╨┐╨░╨┤╨░╨╡╤В ╨╝╨╡╨╢╨┤╤Г ╨╕╤В╨╡╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╤П╨╝╨╕ semaphore.
-
-#### 3.1.2 N+1 ╨▓ `search_facts` тАФ O(results ├Ч facts)

-**╨д╨░╨╣╨╗:** `memory/store.py:97-130`

-```python
-for r in results:                    # ╨║╨░╨╢╨┤╤Л╨╣ ╤А╨╡╨╖╤Г╨╗╤М╤В╨░╤В ╨▓╨╡╨║╤В╨╛╤А╨░
-    for f in self.list_facts("active"):  # ╨Я╨Х╨а╨Х╨з╨Ш╨в╨л╨Т╨Р╨Х╨в ╨Т╨б╨Х ╤Д╨░╨║╤В╤Л ╨╕╨╖ ╨С╨Ф!
-        if f.fact == r["content"]:
-            ...
-```
-
-╨Я╤А╨╕ 20 ╤А╨╡╨╖╤Г╨╗╤М╤В╨░╤В╨░╤Е ╨┐╨╛╨╕╤Б╨║╨░ ╨╕ 1000 ╤Д╨░╨║╤В╨╛╨▓ = 20 ├Ч 1000 = **20 000 ╤Б╤А╨░╨▓╨╜╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╣ + 20 ╨╛╤В╨┤╨╡╨╗╤М╨╜╤Л╤Е SQL-╨╖╨░╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б╨╛╨▓**. ╨б╨░╨╝╤Л╨╣ ╤В╤П╨╢╤С╨╗╤Л╨╣ ╨▒╨░╨│ ╨▓ ╨║╨╛╨┤╨╛╨▓╨╛╨╣ ╨▒╨░╨╖╨╡.

-#### 3.1.3 ╨Ю╤В╤Б╤Г╤В╤Б╤В╨▓╨╕╨╡ timeout ╤Г `oneshot()` ╨▓ compress pipeline
-
-**╨д╨░╨╣╨╗:** `llm/pipeline.py` тАФ ╤Д╤Г╨╜╨║╤Ж╨╕╨╕ `extract_facts`, `consolidate_facts`, `generate_reflections`, `generate_personality_snapshot` тАФ ╨▓╤Б╨╡ ╨▓╤Л╨╖╤Л╨▓╨░╤О╤В `llm.oneshot()` ╨╜╨░╨┐╤А╤П╨╝╤Г╤О, **╨╝╨╕╨╜╤Г╤П** `run_llm()` wrapper.
-
-╨Ю╨┤╨╕╨╜ compress pipeline ╨╝╨╛╨╢╨╡╤В ╨▓╤Л╨┐╨╛╨╗╨╜╨╕╤В╤М **5+ ╨┐╨╛╤Б╨╗╨╡╨┤╨╛╨▓╨░╤В╨╡╨╗╤М╨╜╤Л╤Е LLM-╨▓╤Л╨╖╨╛╨▓╨╛╨▓** ╨▒╨╡╨╖ timeout. ╨Х╤Б╨╗╨╕ Gemini API ╨╖╨░╨▓╨╕╤Б╨╜╨╡╤В тАФ ╨▓╨╡╤Б╤М ╨┐╨░╨╣╨┐╨╗╨░╨╣╨╜ ╨╖╨░╨▒╨╗╨╛╨║╨╕╤А╤Г╨╡╤В╤Б╤П ╨╜╨░╨▓╨╡╤З╨╜╨╛.

-#### 3.1.4 ╨Ю╤В╤Б╤Г╤В╤Б╤В╨▓╨╕╨╡ timeout ╤Г `analyze_message()`

-**╨д╨░╨╣╨╗:** `llm/analyzer.py:38`

-```python

-```

-╨Т╤Л╨╖╤Л╨▓╨░╨╡╤В╤Б╤П ╨▓ **╨│╨╛╤А╤П╤З╨╡╨╝ ╨┐╤Г╤В╨╕** ╨╛╨▒╤А╨░╨▒╨╛╤В╨║╨╕ ╨║╨░╨╢╨┤╨╛╨│╨╛ ╤Б╨╛╨╛╨▒╤Й╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П (╤З╨╡╤А╨╡╨╖ `asyncio.to_thread`, ╨╜╨╛ ╤Б╨░╨╝ `oneshot` ╨▒╨╡╨╖ timeout). ╨Ч╨░╨▓╨╕╤Б╨░╨╜╨╕╨╡ API = ╨▒╨╛╤В ╨┐╨╡╤А╨╡╤Б╤В╨░╤С╤В ╨╛╤В╨▓╨╡╤З╨░╤В╤М.

-## 4. ╨а╨╡╨║╨╛╨╝╨╡╨╜╨┤╤Г╨╡╨╝╤Л╨╡ ╤Д╨░╨╖╤Л ╨╝╨╕╨│╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╨╕ V2

-> ╨д╨╛╨║╤Г╤Б: **╨▒╤Н╨║╨╡╨╜╨┤-╨╛╨┐╤В╨╕╨╝╨╕╨╖╨░╤Ж╨╕╤П ╨╕ ╤Г╤Б╤В╤А╨░╨╜╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╨║╤А╨╕╤В╨╕╤З╨╡╤Б╨║╨╕╤Е ╨┐╤А╨╛╨▒╨╗╨╡╨╝**. ╨д╤А╨╛╨╜╤В╨╡╨╜╨┤ (command surface) ╤Г╨╢╨╡ ╨▓ ╤Е╨╛╤А╨╛╤И╨╡╨╝ ╤Б╨╛╤Б╤В╨╛╤П╨╜╨╕╨╕ ╨┐╨╛╤Б╨╗╨╡ ╤А╨╡╤Д╨░╨║╤В╨╛╤А╨╕╨╜╨│╨░.
-
-### Phase 1: ╨Ш╤Б╨┐╤А╨░╨▓╨╗╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╤Г╤В╨╡╤З╨║╨╕ ╤Д╨╛╨╜╨╛╨▓╤Л╤Е ╨╖╨░╨┤╨░╤З ╨▓ `main.py`

-╨Ч╨░╨╝╨╡╨╜╨╕╤В╤М fire-and-forget `asyncio.create_task()` ╨╜╨░ managed task registry:

-                await coro

-### Phase 2: ╨Я╨░╨║╨╡╤В╨╜╨░╤П ╨▓╤Б╤В╨░╨▓╨║╨░ ╨╕ connection pooling ╨▓ `sqlite_db.py`

-#### 2.2 Batch insert ╨╝╨╡╤В╨╛╨┤╤Л

-╨Ф╨╛╨▒╨░╨▓╨╕╤В╤М ╨┐╨░╨║╨╡╤В╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╨▓╨░╤А╨╕╨░╨╜╤В╤Л ╨┤╨╗╤П ╨╝╨░╤Б╤Б╨╛╨▓╤Л╤Е ╨╛╨┐╨╡╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╨╣:

-def batch_insert_facts(self, rows: list[dict]) -> None:

-        conn.executemany(
-            "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO facts VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",

-        )
-
-def batch_insert_messages(self, rows: list[dict]) -> None:

-            "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO messages VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
-            [self._message_tuple(r) for r in rows]

-```
-
-#### 2.3 ╨а╨╡╤Д╨░╨║╤В╨╛╤А╨╕╨╜╨│ JSONL-╨╝╨╕╨│╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╨╕
-
-╨в╨╡╨║╤Г╤Й╨╕╨╣ `_migrate_jsonl_if_empty` ╨▓╤Л╨╖╤Л╨▓╨░╨╡╤В `_insert_fact` (╤Б ╨╜╨╛╨▓╤Л╨╝ connection) ╨┤╨╗╤П ╨║╨░╨╢╨┤╨╛╨╣ ╤Б╤В╤А╨╛╨║╨╕. ╨Ч╨░╨╝╨╡╨╜╨╕╤В╤М ╨╜╨░ batch:
-
-```python

-| `RetrievalBudgetManager` | ╨С╤О╨┤╨╢╨╡╤В╨╕╤А╨╛╨▓╨░╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╣ retrieval ╤Б ╨┐╤А╨╕╨╛╤А╨╕╤В╨╡╤В╨░╨╝╨╕, pinned facts, trimming тАФ ╤Е╨╛╤А╨╛╤И╨╛ ╤А╨░╨▒╨╛╤В╨░╨╡╤В |

-## 8. Executive Summary V2
-
-╨Я╤А╨╛╨╡╨║╤В ╨┐╤А╨╛╤И╤С╨╗ ╤З╨╡╤А╨╡╨╖ **╤Г╤Б╨┐╨╡╤И╨╜╤Л╨╣ ╤А╨╡╤Д╨░╨║╤В╨╛╤А╨╕╨╜╨│ ╤Д╤А╨╛╨╜╤В╨╡╨╜╨┤╨░**: command surface ╤З╨╕╤Б╤В╤Л╨╣, ╨║╨╛╨│╨╜╨╕╤В╨╕╨▓╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╤Б╨╗╨╛╨╕ ╨╕╨╜╤В╨╡╨│╤А╨╕╤А╨╛╨▓╨░╨╜╤Л, ╨╝╤Г╤Б╨╛╤А╨╜╤Л╨╣ ╨║╨╛╨┤ ╤Г╨┤╨░╨╗╤С╨╜. ╨С╨╛╤В ╤А╨░╨▒╨╛╤В╨░╨╡╤В ╨║╨░╨║ AI-companion, ╨░ ╨╜╨╡ ╨║╨░╨║ toolbox.
-
-**╨з╤В╨╛ ╨╛╤Б╤В╨░╨╗╨╛╤Б╤М тАФ ╤Н╤В╨╛ ╨▒╤Н╨║╨╡╨╜╨┤-╨╛╨┐╤В╨╕╨╝╨╕╨╖╨░╤Ж╨╕╤П:**
-
-1. **╨г╤В╨╡╤З╨║╨╕ ╨╖╨░╨┤╨░╤З** тАФ fire-and-forget tasks ╨▒╨╡╨╖ ╨╛╤В╨╝╨╡╨╜╤Л ╨┐╤А╨╕ shutdown
-2. **SQLite connection churn** тАФ ╨╜╨╛╨▓╨╛╨╡ ╤Б╨╛╨╡╨┤╨╕╨╜╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╨╜╨░ ╨║╨░╨╢╨┤╤Г╤О ╨╛╨┐╨╡╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╤О, ╨╜╨╡╤В batch insert, ╨╜╨╡╤В WAL
-3. **LLM timeout holes** тАФ 6+ ╨╝╨╡╤Б╤В ╨│╨┤╨╡ `oneshot()` ╨▓╤Л╨╖╤Л╨▓╨░╨╡╤В╤Б╤П ╨▒╨╡╨╖ timeout, ╨▓╨║╨╗╤О╤З╨░╤П ╨│╨╛╤А╤П╤З╨╕╨╣ ╨┐╤Г╤В╤М ╨░╨╜╨░╨╗╨╕╨╖╨░ ╤Б╨╛╨╛╨▒╤Й╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╣
-4. **N+1 ╨╖╨░╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б╤Л** тАФ `search_facts` ╨┐╨╡╤А╨╡╤З╨╕╤В╤Л╨▓╨░╨╡╤В ╨▓╤Б╨╡ ╤Д╨░╨║╤В╤Л ╨┤╨╗╤П ╨║╨░╨╢╨┤╨╛╨│╨╛ ╤А╨╡╨╖╤Г╨╗╤М╤В╨░╤В╨░ vector search
-5. **User model тАФ write-only** тАФ ╨╡╨┤╨╕╨╜╤Б╤В╨▓╨╡╨╜╨╜╨░╤П ╨║╤А╤Г╨┐╨╜╨░╤П ╨║╨╛╨│╨╜╨╕╤В╨╕╨▓╨╜╨░╤П ╤Д╨╕╤З╨░, ╨║╨╛╤В╨╛╤А╨░╤П ╨Э╨Х ╨┐╨╛╨┐╨░╨┤╨░╨╡╤В ╨▓ ╨┐╤А╨╛╨╝╨┐╤В
-
-╨Т╤Б╨╡ ╤Н╤В╨╕ ╨┐╤А╨╛╨▒╨╗╨╡╨╝╤Л ╤А╨╡╤И╨░╨╡╨╝╤Л ╨▒╨╡╨╖ ╨╕╨╖╨╝╨╡╨╜╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П ╨┐╤Г╨▒╨╗╨╕╤З╨╜╨╛╨│╨╛ API ╨▒╨╛╤В╨░. ╨д╨░╨╖╤Л 1-3 (╨║╤А╨╕╤В╨╕╤З╨╡╤Б╨║╨╕╨╡) ╨╝╨╛╨╢╨╜╨╛ ╤А╨╡╨░╨╗╨╕╨╖╨╛╨▓╨░╤В╤М ╨┐╨░╤А╨░╨╗╨╗╨╡╨╗╤М╨╜╨╛.

-## 1. ╨Р╤А╤Е╨╕╤В╨╡╨║╤В╤Г╤А╨╜╤Л╨╣ ╨╛╨▒╨╖╨╛╤А (High-Level)
-
-╨Я╤А╨╛╨╡╨║╤В ╨┐╨╛╤Б╤В╤А╨╛╨╡╨╜ ╨┐╨╛ ╨╝╨╛╨┤╤Г╨╗╤М╨╜╨╛╨╝╤Г ╨┐╤А╨╕╨╜╤Ж╨╕╨┐╤Г ╤Б ╤З╨╡╤В╨║╨╕╨╝ ╤А╨░╨╖╨┤╨╡╨╗╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╡╨╝ ╨╜╨░ ╤Б╨╗╨╛╨╕: ╨┐╤А╨╕╨╡╨╝ ╤Б╨╛╨╛╨▒╤Й╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╣ (handlers), ╨▒╨╕╨╖╨╜╨╡╤Б-╨╗╨╛╨│╨╕╨║╨░ (services), ╨║╨╛╨│╨╜╨╕╤В╨╕╨▓╨╜╨░╤П ╨╛╨▒╤А╨░╨▒╨╛╤В╨║╨░ (core, reasoning, policy, self/user models), ╨╕╨╜╤В╨╡╨│╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╤П ╤Б LLM (llm), ╤Г╨┐╤А╨░╨▓╨╗╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╨┐╨░╨╝╤П╤В╤М╤О (memory) ╨╕ ╨╜╨╕╨╖╨║╨╛╤Г╤А╨╛╨▓╨╜╨╡╨▓╨╛╨╡ ╤Е╤А╨░╨╜╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╨┤╨░╨╜╨╜╤Л╤Е (storage).
-
-*   **`companion/`** тАФ ╨╛╤Б╨╜╨╛╨▓╨╜╨╛╨╡ ╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б╤В╤А╨░╨╜╤Б╤В╨▓╨╛ ╨╕╨╝╨╡╨╜ ╨║╨╛╨┤╨╛╨▓╨╛╨╣ ╨▒╨░╨╖╤Л ╨▒╨╛╤В╨░.
-*   **`companion/handlers/`** тАФ ╤В╤А╨░╨╜╤Б╨┐╨╛╤А╤В╨╜╤Л╨╣ ╤Б╨╗╨╛╨╣ (╨╕╨╜╤В╨╡╨│╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╤П ╤Б aiogram). ╨Я╤А╨╕╨╜╨╕╨╝╨░╨╡╤В ╤В╨╡╨║╤Б╤В╨╛╨▓╤Л╨╡, ╨╝╨╡╨┤╨╕╨░- ╨╕ callback-╨╖╨░╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б╤Л ╨╕╨╖ Telegram ╨╕ ╨╜╨░╨┐╤А╨░╨▓╨╗╤П╨╡╤В ╨╕╤Е ╨┤╨░╨╗╤М╤И╨╡.
-*   **`companion/services/`** тАФ ╤Б╨╡╤А╨▓╨╕╤Б╨╜╤Л╨╣ ╤Б╨╗╨╛╨╣, ╨╕╨╜╨║╨░╨┐╤Б╤Г╨╗╨╕╤А╤Г╤О╤Й╨╕╨╣ ╨╗╨╛╨│╨╕╨║╤Г ╨▓╤Л╨┐╨╛╨╗╨╜╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П ╨┐╨╛╨╗╤М╨╖╨╛╨▓╨░╤В╨╡╨╗╤М╤Б╨║╨╕╤Е ╨║╨╛╨╝╨░╨╜╨┤ (╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б╨╝╨╛╤В╤А ╤Ж╨╡╨╗╨╡╨╣, ╤Н╨║╤Б╨┐╨╛╤А╤В ╨┤╨╜╨╡╨▓╨╜╨╕╨║╨░, ╤Д╨╛╤А╨╝╨░╤В╨╕╤А╨╛╨▓╨░╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╨╖╨░╨┤╨░╤З ╨╕ ╨╛╤В╤З╨╡╤В╨╛╨▓). ╨Т╤Л╨╖╤Л╨▓╨░╨╡╤В╤Б╤П ╨╗╨╕╨▒╨╛ ╤П╨▓╨╜╨╛ ╤З╨╡╤А╨╡╨╖ slash-╨║╨╛╨╝╨░╨╜╨┤╤Л, ╨╗╨╕╨▒╨╛ ╨░╨▓╤В╨╛╨╝╨░╤В╨╕╤З╨╡╤Б╨║╨╕ ╨┐╨╛ ╤А╨░╤Б╨┐╨╛╨╖╨╜╨░╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╝ ╤В╨╡╨║╤Б╤В╨╛╨▓╤Л╨╝ ╨╕╨╜╤В╨╡╨╜╤В╨░╨╝.
-*   **`companion/llm/`** тАФ ╨░╨┤╨░╨┐╤В╨╡╤А╤Л ╨╕ ╨┐╨░╨╣╨┐╨╗╨░╨╣╨╜╤Л ╨▓╨╖╨░╨╕╨╝╨╛╨┤╨╡╨╣╤Б╤В╨▓╨╕╤П ╤Б Google Gemini API. ╨Ю╤В╨▓╨╡╤З╨░╨╡╤В ╨╖╨░ ╨▓╤Л╨╖╨╛╨▓╤Л ╨╝╨╛╨┤╨╡╨╗╨╕, ╤А╨░╨╖╨╝╨╡╤В╨║╤Г ╨┐╤А╨╛╨╝╨┐╤В╨╛╨▓, ╨░╨╜╨░╨╗╨╕╨╖ ╨╕╨╜╤В╨╡╨╜╤В╨╛╨▓, ╨│╨╡╨╜╨╡╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╤О ╤Б╨░╨╝╨╝╨░╤А╨╕ ╨╕ ╨╖╨░╨╖╨╡╨╝╨╗╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╡ (grounding) ╨▓ ╨┐╨╛╨╕╤Б╨║╨╛╨▓╤Г╤О ╨▓╤Л╨┤╨░╤З╤Г.
-*   **`companion/memory/`** тАФ ╨║╨╛╨│╨╜╨╕╤В╨╕╨▓╨╜╨░╤П ╨┐╨░╨╝╤П╤В╤М. ╨г╨┐╤А╨░╨▓╨╗╤П╨╡╤В ╨▓╨╡╨║╤В╨╛╤А╨╜╤Л╨╝ ╨╕╨╜╨┤╨╡╨║╤Б╨╛╨╝, ╤А╨░╨╜╨╢╨╕╤А╨╛╨▓╨░╨╜╨╕╨╡╨╝ ╤Д╨░╨║╤В╨╛╨▓ ╨┐╨╛ ╨▓╨░╨╢╨╜╨╛╤Б╤В╨╕, ╤А╨╡╨╗╨╡╨▓╨░╨╜╤В╨╜╨╛╤Б╤В╨╕ ╨╕ ╨▓╤А╨╡╨╝╨╡╨╜╨╕, ╨░ ╤В╨░╨║╨╢╨╡ ╤А╨░╤Б╤З╨╡╤В╨╛╨╝ ╨▒╤О╨┤╨╢╨╡╤В╨░ ╨║╨╛╨╜╤В╨╡╨║╤Б╤В╨░.
-*   **`companion/storage/`** тАФ ╤Б╨╗╨╛╨╣ ╨┐╨╡╤А╤Б╨╕╤Б╤В╨╡╨╜╤В╨╜╨╛╤Б╤В╨╕. ╨а╨╡╨░╨╗╨╕╨╖╤Г╨╡╤В ╨│╨╕╨▒╤А╨╕╨┤╨╜╨╛╨╡ ╤Е╤А╨░╨╜╨╕╨╗╨╕╤Й╨╡: ╨▒╨░╨╖╨░ ╨┤╨░╨╜╨╜╤Л╤Е SQLite (╨╛╤Б╨╜╨╛╨▓╨╜╨╛╨╡ ╤Е╤А╨░╨╜╨╕╨╗╨╕╤Й╨╡ ╨▓ ╤А╨╡╨╢╨╕╨╝╨╡ WAL) ╨╕ ╤В╨╡╨║╤Б╤В╨╛╨▓╤Л╨╡ ╨╖╨╡╤А╨║╨░╨╗╨░ ╨▓ ╤Д╨╛╤А╨╝╨░╤В╨╡ JSONL.
-*   **`data/`** тАФ ╤Б╨╛╨╖╨┤╨░╨▓╨░╨╡╨╝╨░╤П ╨┐╤А╨╕ ╨┐╨╡╤А╨▓╨╛╨╝ ╨╖╨░╨┐╤Г╤Б╨║╨╡ ╨┤╨╕╤А╨╡╨║╤В╨╛╤А╨╕╤П ╨┤╨╗╤П ╤Е╤А╨░╨╜╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П ╨╗╨╛╨║╨░╨╗╤М╨╜╨╛╨╣ ╨▒╨░╨╖╤Л ╨┤╨░╨╜╨╜╤Л╤Е SQLite (`companion.db`) ╨╕ JSONL ╨╗╨╛╨│╨╛╨▓.
-

-## 2. ╨Ш╨╜╨▓╨╡╨╜╤В╨░╤А╨╕╨╖╨░╤Ж╨╕╤П ╤Д╨░╨╣╨╗╨╛╨▓
-
-### ╨Ъ╨╛╤А╨╡╨╜╤М ╨┐╤А╨╛╨╡╨║╤В╨░ ╨╕ ╤Д╨░╨╣╨╗╤Л ╨║╨╛╨╜╤Д╨╕╨│╤Г╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╨╕
-*   **[bot.py](file:///C:/Games/bot.py)**: ╨У╨╗╨░╨▓╨╜╨░╤П ╤В╨╛╤З╨║╨░ ╨▓╤Е╨╛╨┤╨░ ╨┤╨╗╤П ╨╖╨░╨┐╤Г╤Б╨║╨░ ╨▒╨╛╤В╨░. ╨Ш╨╝╨┐╨╛╤А╤В╨╕╤А╤Г╨╡╤В ╤Д╤Г╨╜╨║╤Ж╨╕╤О `main()` ╨╕╨╖ ╨┐╨░╨║╨╡╤В╨░ `companion.main` ╨╕ ╨╖╨░╨┐╤Г╤Б╨║╨░╨╡╤В ╨╡╨╡. ╨Э╤Г╨╢╨╡╨╜ ╨╕╤Б╨║╨╗╤О╤З╨╕╤В╨╡╨╗╤М╨╜╨╛ ╨║╨░╨║ ╨╗╨╡╨│╨║╨╛╨▓╨╡╤Б╨╜╤Л╨╣ ╤Б╨║╤А╨╕╨┐╤В ╨┤╨╗╤П ╨╖╨░╨┐╤Г╤Б╨║╨░ ╨╕╨╖ ╨║╨╛╨╝╨░╨╜╨┤╨╜╨╛╨╣ ╤Б╤В╤А╨╛╨║╨╕.
-*   **[api.env](file:///C:/Games/api.env)**: ╨д╨░╨╣╨╗ ╨║╨╛╨╜╤Д╨╕╨│╤Г╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╨╕ ╨┐╨╡╤А╨╡╨╝╨╡╨╜╨╜╤Л╤Е ╨╛╨║╤А╤Г╨╢╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П (╨▓ ╨║╨╛╨┤╨╡ ╨╖╨░╨│╤А╤Г╨╢╨░╨╡╤В╤Б╤П ╤З╨╡╤А╨╡╨╖ `dotenv`). ╨е╤А╨░╨╜╨╕╤В ╤В╨╛╨║╨╡╨╜ Telegram-╨▒╨╛╤В╨░ (`API_TOKEN`), API-╨║╨╗╤О╤З Gemini (`GOOGLE_API_KEY`), ID ╨░╨┤╨╝╨╕╨╜╨╕╤Б╤В╤А╨░╤В╨╛╤А╨╛╨▓ (`ADMIN_IDS`), ╨╜╨░╤Б╤В╤А╨╛╨╣╨║╨╕ ╨╗╨╛╨│╨╕╤А╨╛╨▓╨░╨╜╨╕╤П ╨╕ ╤Г╤А╨╛╨▓╨╜╨╕ ╤Д╨╕╨╗╤М╤В╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╨╕ ╨║╨╛╨╜╤В╨╡╨╜╤В╨░.
-*   **[pyproject.toml](file:///C:/Games/pyproject.toml)**: ╨Ъ╨╛╨╜╤Д╨╕╨│╤Г╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╨╛╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╣ ╤Д╨░╨╣╨╗ ╨┐╤А╨╛╨╡╨║╤В╨░ ╨┤╨╗╤П Python-╨╕╨╜╤Б╤В╤А╤Г╨╝╨╡╨╜╤В╨╛╨▓. ╨Ч╨░╨┤╨░╨╡╤В ╨╜╨░╤Б╤В╤А╨╛╨╣╨║╨╕ ╨╗╨╕╨╜╤В╨╡╤А╨░ Ruff ╨╕ ╨┐╨░╤А╨░╨╝╨╡╤В╤А╤Л ╤В╨╡╤Б╤В╨╛╨▓╨╛╨╣ ╤Б╤А╨╡╨┤╤Л Pytest.
-*   **[requirements.txt](file:///C:/Games/requirements.txt)**: ╨б╨┐╨╕╤Б╨╛╨║ ╨▓╨╜╨╡╤И╨╜╨╕╤Е ╨╖╨░╨▓╨╕╤Б╨╕╨╝╨╛╤Б╤В╨╡╨╣ ╨┐╤А╨╛╨╡╨║╤В╨░ (╨▓╨║╨╗╤О╤З╨░╤П `aiogram`, `google-genai`, `speechrecognition`, `pydub`, `yt-dlp`, `pytest` ╨╕ ╨┤╤А.).
-*   **[.gitignore](file:///C:/Games/.gitignore)**: ╨Ш╤Б╨║╨╗╤О╤З╨░╨╡╤В ╨╕╨╖ ╤А╨╡╨┐╨╛╨╖╨╕╤В╨╛╤А╨╕╤П ╨╗╨╛╨║╨░╨╗╤М╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╨║╤Н╤И╨╕ (`__pycache__`, `.pytest_cache`), ╨╗╨╛╨│╨╕, ╤Д╨░╨╣╨╗╤Л ╨╜╨░╤Б╤В╤А╨╛╨╡╨║ IDE (`.vscode`), ╨░ ╤В╨░╨║╨╢╨╡ ╨┐╨╡╤А╤Б╨╛╨╜╨░╨╗╤М╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╨┤╨░╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╨╕ ╨▒╨░╨╖╤Л ╨┤╨░╨╜╨╜╤Л╤Е ╨╕╨╖ ╨┐╨░╨┐╨║╨╕ `data/`.
-
-### companion/ (╨Ъ╨╛╤А╨╡╨╜╤М ╨╝╨╛╨┤╤Г╨╗╤П)
-*   **[companion/\_\_init\_\_.py](file:///C:/Games/companion/__init__.py)**: ╨Ш╨╜╨╕╤Ж╨╕╨░╨╗╨╕╨╖╨╕╤А╤Г╨╡╤В ╨╝╨╛╨┤╤Г╨╗╤М `companion` ╨╕ ╤Н╨║╤Б╨┐╨╛╤А╤В╨╕╤А╤Г╨╡╤В ╨▓╨╡╤А╤Б╨╕╤О ╨┐╨░╨║╨╡╤В╨░.
-*   **[companion/main.py](file:///C:/Games/companion/main.py)**: ╨в╨╛╤З╨║╨░ ╤Б╨▒╨╛╤А╨║╨╕ ╨┐╤А╨╕╨╗╨╛╨╢╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П. ╨Ю╤В╨▓╨╡╤З╨░╨╡╤В ╨╖╨░ ╨╕╨╜╨╕╤Ж╨╕╨░╨╗╨╕╨╖╨░╤Ж╨╕╤О ╨╗╨╛╨│╨╕╤А╨╛╨▓╨░╨╜╨╕╤П, ╤А╨╛╤В╨░╤Ж╨╕╤О ╤Б╤Л╤А╤Л╤Е ╤Д╨░╨╣╨╗╨╛╨▓, ╨┐╤А╨╛╨│╤А╨╡╨▓ ╨▓╨╡╨║╤В╨╛╤А╨╜╨╛╨│╨╛ ╨╕╨╜╨┤╨╡╨║╤Б╨░ SQLite, ╤А╨╡╨│╨╕╤Б╤В╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╤О middlewares (╨╜╨░╨┐╤А╨╕╨╝╨╡╤А, `AuthMiddleware` ╨┤╨╗╤П ╨░╨▓╤В╨╛╤А╨╕╨╖╨░╤Ж╨╕╨╕ ╨Ш╨▓╨░╨╜╨░), ╨╖╨░╨┐╤Г╤Б╨║ ╨▒╨╡╤Б╨║╨╛╨╜╨╡╤З╨╜╨╛╨│╨╛ ╤Ж╨╕╨║╨╗╨░ proactive-╨┐╨╕╨╜╨│╨░ ╨╕ graceful shutdown (╨╛╤В╨╝╨╡╨╜╤Г ╨▓╤Б╨╡╤Е ╤Д╨╛╨╜╨╛╨▓╤Л╤Е ╨╖╨░╨┤╨░╤З ╨┐╤А╨╕ ╨▓╤Л╤Е╨╛╨┤╨╡). ╨Т╨╖╨░╨╕╨╝╨╛╨┤╨╡╨╣╤Б╤В╨▓╤Г╨╡╤В ╤Б `handlers`, `bot_core` ╨╕ `background_scheduler`.
-*   **[companion/bot_core.py](file:///C:/Games/companion/bot_core.py)**: ╨Ю╤А╨║╨╡╤Б╤В╤А╨░╤В╨╛╤А ╤П╨┤╤А╨░. ╨Ъ╨╛╨╛╤А╨┤╨╕╨╜╨╕╤А╤Г╨╡╤В ╤Б╨║╨▓╨╛╨╖╨╜╨╛╨╣ ╨╢╨╕╨╖╨╜╨╡╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╣ ╤Ж╨╕╨║╨╗ ╨╛╨▒╤А╨░╨▒╨╛╤В╨║╨╕ ╨╖╨░╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б╨░: ╨╗╨╕╨╝╨╕╤В╤Л ╨╖╨░╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б╨╛╨▓ (rate limiter), ╨▓╤Л╨╖╨╛╨▓ LLM-╨░╨╜╨░╨╗╨╕╨╖╨░╤В╨╛╤А╨░, ╨╕╨╖╨▓╨╗╨╡╤З╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╨┐╨░╨╝╤П╤В╨╕, ╨╝╨░╤А╤И╤А╤Г╤В╨╕╨╖╨░╤Ж╨╕╤О ╨║╨╛╨╝╨░╨╜╨┤, ╨▓╤Л╨╖╨╛╨▓ ╤А╨╡╤В╤А╨╕╨▓╨░╨╗╨░ ╨║╨╛╨╜╤В╨╡╨║╤Б╤В╨░, ╨╖╨░╨┐╤Г╤Б╨║ ╨│╨╡╨╜╨╡╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╨╕ ╨╛╤В╨▓╨╡╤В╨░, ╨┐╤А╨╛╨▓╨╡╤А╨║╤Г ╤Ж╨╡╨╜╨╖╤Г╤А╤Л ╨╕ ╨┐╨╛╤Б╤В-╨║╤А╨╕╤В╨╕╨║╤Г. ╨б╨▓╤П╨╖╤Г╤О╤Й╨╡╨╡ ╨╖╨▓╨╡╨╜╨╛ ╨╝╨╡╨╢╨┤╤Г `handlers/chat.py` ╨╕ ╨▓╤Б╨╡╨╝╨╕ ╨║╨╛╨│╨╜╨╕╤В╨╕╨▓╨╜╤Л╨╝╨╕ ╤Б╨╡╤А╨▓╨╕╤Б╨░╨╝╨╕.
-*   **[companion/config.py](file:///C:/Games/companion/config.py)**: ╨Х╨┤╨╕╨╜╤Л╨╣ ╤А╨╡╨╡╤Б╤В╤А ╨║╨╛╨╜╤Б╤В╨░╨╜╤В ╨╕ ╨┐╤Г╤В╨╡╨╣ ╨┐╤А╨╛╨╡╨║╤В╨░. ╨б╤З╨╕╤В╤Л╨▓╨░╨╡╤В ╨┤╨░╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╨╕╨╖ `api.env`, ╨╜╨░╤Б╤В╤А╨░╨╕╨▓╨░╨╡╤В ╨┐╤Г╤В╨╕ ╨║ SQLite ╨╕ JSONL ╤Д╨░╨╣╨╗╨░╨╝, ╨╖╨░╨┤╨░╨╡╤В ╨┤╨╡╤Д╨╛╨╗╤В╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╨╗╨╕╨╝╨╕╤В╤Л ╤Б╨╕╨╝╨▓╨╛╨╗╨╛╨▓ ╨┤╨╗╤П ╨║╨╛╨╜╤В╨╡╨║╤Б╤В╨░ (`RETRIEVAL_CHAR_BUDGET`), ╤В╨░╨╣╨╝╨░╤Г╤В╤Л LLM ╨╕ ╤Г╤А╨╛╨▓╨╜╨╕ ╨▒╨╡╨╖╨╛╨┐╨░╤Б╨╜╨╛╤Б╤В╨╕. ╨Ш╤Б╨┐╨╛╨╗╤М╨╖╤Г╨╡╤В╤Б╤П ╨┐╤А╨░╨║╤В╨╕╤З╨╡╤Б╨║╨╕ ╨║╨░╨╢╨┤╤Л╨╝ ╨╝╨╛╨┤╤Г╨╗╨╡╨╝ ╨▓ ╤Б╨╕╤Б╤В╨╡╨╝╨╡.
-*   **[companion/runtime_state.py](file:///C:/Games/companion/runtime_state.py)**: ╨Ю╨┐╨╕╤Б╨░╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╤Б╤В╤А╤Г╨║╤В╤Г╤А╤Л `RuntimeState`. ╨н╤В╨╛ ╨▓╤А╨╡╨╝╨╡╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╣ ╨║╨╛╨╜╤В╨╡╨╣╨╜╨╡╤А-╨╛╨▒╤К╨╡╨║╤В, ╤Б╨╛╨╖╨┤╨░╨▓╨░╨╡╨╝╤Л╨╣ ╨┐╨╛╨┤ ╨║╨░╨╢╨┤╤Л╨╣ ╨▓╤Е╨╛╨┤╤П╤Й╨╕╨╣ ╨╖╨░╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б ╨┐╨╛╨╗╤М╨╖╨╛╨▓╨░╤В╨╡╨╗╤П. ╨Я╨╡╤А╨╡╨╜╨╛╤Б╨╕╤В ╨┐╤А╨╛╨╝╨╡╨╢╤Г╤В╨╛╤З╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╨╝╨╡╤В╨░╨┤╨░╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╡ (╨╕╨╜╤В╨╡╨╜╤В, ╨▓╨░╨╢╨╜╨╛╤Б╤В╤М, ╨╜╨░╤Б╤В╤А╨╛╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╡, ╨╛╨│╤А╨░╨╜╨╕╤З╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П ╨╛╤В ╨┐╨╛╨╗╨╕╤В╨╕╨║ ╨▒╨╡╨╖╨╛╨┐╨░╤Б╨╜╨╛╤Б╤В╨╕, ╤З╨╡╤А╨╜╨╛╨▓╨╕╨║ ╨╛╤В╨▓╨╡╤В╨░, ╤А╨╡╨╖╤Г╨╗╤М╤В╨░╤В╤Л ╨║╤А╨╕╤В╨╕╨║╨╕) ╨╝╨╡╨╢╨┤╤Г ╤И╨░╨│╨░╨╝╨╕ ╨┐╨░╨╣╨┐╨╗╨░╨╣╨╜╨░ ╨▓ `bot_core.py`.
-*   **[companion/models.py](file:///C:/Games/companion/models.py)**: ╨Ю╨┐╨╕╤Б╨░╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╨╛╤Б╨╜╨╛╨▓╨╜╤Л╤Е ╤Б╤Г╤Й╨╜╨╛╤Б╤В╨╡╨╣ ╨┐╤А╨╡╨┤╨╝╨╡╤В╨╜╨╛╨╣ ╨╛╨▒╨╗╨░╤Б╤В╨╕ ╨▓ ╨▓╨╕╨┤╨╡ dataclass-╨╛╨▓: `Fact` (╨▓╨╛╤Б╨┐╨╛╨╝╨╕╨╜╨░╨╜╨╕╨╡/╤Д╨░╨║╤В), `FactRelation` (╤Б╨▓╤П╨╖╨╕ ╨╝╨╡╨╢╨┤╤Г ╤Д╨░╨║╤В╨░╨╝╨╕), `MessageRecord` (╨╖╨░╨┐╨╕╤Б╤М ╤Б╨╛╨╛╨▒╤Й╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П ╨▓ ╨С╨Ф), `Reflection` (╨▓╤Л╨▓╨╛╨┤/╨╕╨╜╤Б╨░╨╣╤В ╨╛ ╨┐╨╛╨╗╤М╨╖╨╛╨▓╨░╤В╨╡╨╗╨╡) ╨╕ `ContextBundle` (╨│╨╛╤В╨╛╨▓╤Л╨╣ ╤Б╤А╨╡╨╖ ╨║╨╛╨╜╤В╨╡╨║╤Б╤В╨░ ╨┤╨╗╤П ╨╕╨╜╤К╨╡╨║╤Ж╨╕╨╕ ╨▓ ╨┐╤А╨╛╨╝╨┐╤В).
-*   **[companion/background_scheduler.py](file:///C:/Games/companion/background_scheduler.py)**: ╨г╨┐╤А╨░╨▓╨╗╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╤Д╨╛╨╜╨╛╨▓╤Л╨╝╨╕ ╨╖╨░╨┤╨░╤З╨░╨╝╨╕. ╨Я╤А╨╡╨┤╨╛╤Б╤В╨░╨▓╨╗╤П╨╡╤В ╨┐╨╛╤В╨╛╨║╨╛╨▒╨╡╨╖╨╛╨┐╨░╤Б╨╜╤Л╨╣ ╨╗╨╕╨╝╨╕╤В╨╕╤А╨╛╨▓╨░╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╣ ╨╖╨░╨┐╤Г╤Б╨║ ╨░╤Б╨╕╨╜╤Е╤А╨╛╨╜╨╜╤Л╤Е ╨╖╨░╨┤╨░╤З (`safe_task` ╤Б ╤Б╨╡╨╝╨░╤Д╨╛╤А╨╛╨╝ ╨╜╨░ 5 ╤И╤В╤Г╨║), ╨╗╨╛╨│╨╕╤А╨╛╨▓╨░╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╨╕╤Е ╨┐╨░╨┤╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╣ ╨▓ `self_model` ╨╕ ╨╝╨╡╤Е╨░╨╜╨╕╨╖╨╝ ╨┐╤А╨╡╨┤╨╛╤Е╤А╨░╨╜╨╕╤В╨╡╨╗╤П (circuit breaker), ╨▓╤А╨╡╨╝╨╡╨╜╨╜╨╛ ╨╛╤В╨║╨╗╤О╤З╨░╤О╤Й╨╕╨╣ ╨┐╨░╨┤╨░╤О╤Й╨╕╨╡ ╤В╨╕╨┐╤Л ╨╖╨░╨┤╨░╤З ╨┐╤А╨╕ ╤З╨░╤Б╤В╤Л╤Е ╤Б╨▒╨╛╤П╤Е. ╨в╨░╨║╨╢╨╡ ╤Б╨╛╨┤╨╡╤А╨╢╨╕╤В ╤Д╤Г╨╜╨║╤Ж╨╕╨╕ ╤Д╨╛╨╜╨╛╨▓╨╛╨│╨╛ ╨╛╨▒╨╜╨╛╨▓╨╗╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П ╨┐╤А╨╛╤Д╨╕╨╗╤П ╨Ш╨▓╨░╨╜╨░.
-*   **[companion/critique_manager.py](file:///C:/Games/companion/critique_manager.py)**: ╨Ь╨╡╤В╨░-╨╝╨╛╨╜╨╕╤В╨╛╤А╨╕╨╜╨│ ╨╛╤В╨▓╨╡╤В╨╛╨▓. ╨Ч╨░╨┐╤Г╤Б╨║╨░╨╡╤В `SelfModel` ╨┤╨╗╤П ╨║╤А╨╕╤В╨╕╨║╨╕ ╤Б╨│╨╡╨╜╨╡╤А╨╕╤А╨╛╨▓╨░╨╜╨╜╨╛╨│╨╛ Gemini ╤З╨╡╤А╨╜╨╛╨▓╨╕╨║╨░ ╨╛╤В╨▓╨╡╤В╨░. ╨Х╤Б╨╗╨╕ ╤Г╨▓╨╡╤А╨╡╨╜╨╜╨╛╤Б╤В╤М ╨▒╨╛╤В╨░ ╨╜╨╕╨╢╨╡ 55% ╨╕╨╗╨╕ ╨╛╨▒╨╜╨░╤А╤Г╨╢╨╡╨╜╤Л ╨╜╨╡╨┐╨╛╨┤╤В╨▓╨╡╤А╨╢╨┤╨╡╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╤Д╨░╨║╤В╤Л, ╨┤╨╛╨▒╨░╨▓╨╗╤П╨╡╤В ╨╝╨░╤А╨║╨╡╤А╤Л ╤Б╨╛╨╝╨╜╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П (┬л╨Э╨╡ ╤Г╨▓╨╡╤А╨╡╨╜...┬╗) ╨╕╨╗╨╕ ╨┐╨╡╤А╨╡╨╜╨░╨┐╤А╨░╨▓╨╗╤П╨╡╤В ╨╖╨░╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б ╨▓╨╛ ╨▓╨╜╨╡╤И╨╜╨╕╨╣ ╨┐╨╛╨╕╤Б╨║ (grounding).
-*   **[companion/policy_layer.py](file:///C:/Games/companion/policy_layer.py)**: ╨б╨╗╨╛╨╣ ╨┐╨╛╨▓╨╡╨┤╨╡╨╜╤З╨╡╤Б╨║╨╛╨╣ ╨┐╨╛╨╗╨╕╤В╨╕╨║╨╕. ╨Э╨░ ╨╛╤Б╨╜╨╛╨▓╨╡ ╨╜╨░╤Б╤В╤А╨╛╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П ╨Ш╨▓╨░╨╜╨░ (╨╜╨░╨┐╤А╨╕╨╝╨╡╤А, `anxious` ╨╕╨╗╨╕ `depressed`) ╤Б╨╛╨┐╨╛╤Б╤В╨░╨▓╨╗╤П╨╡╤В ╤В╨╡╨║╤Г╤Й╤Г╤О ╤Б╨╕╤В╤Г╨░╤Ж╨╕╤О ╤Б ╨╜╨░╨▒╨╛╤А╨╛╨╝ ╨┐╤А╨░╨▓╨╕╨╗: ╤Б╨╛╨║╤А╨░╤В╨╕╤В╤М ╨┤╨╗╨╕╨╜╤Г ╤В╨╡╨║╤Б╤В╨░, ╨╜╨╡ ╨╖╨░╨┤╨░╨▓╨░╤В╤М ╨╗╨╕╤И╨╜╨╕╤Е ╨▓╨╛╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б╨╛╨▓, ╨╕╤Б╨║╨╗╤О╤З╨╕╤В╤М ╤В╨╡╨╛╤А╨╡╤В╨╕╨╖╨╕╤А╨╛╨▓╨░╨╜╨╕╨╡. ╨Я╤А╨╕╨╝╨╡╨╜╤П╨╡╤В ╨╛╨│╤А╨░╨╜╨╕╤З╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П ╨║╨░╨║ ╨╜╨░ ╤Н╤В╨░╨┐╨╡ ╤Б╨▒╨╛╤А╨║╨╕ ╨┐╤А╨╛╨╝╨┐╤В╨░ (`format_prompt_with_policy`), ╤В╨░╨║ ╨╕ ╨╜╨░ ╤Н╤В╨░╨┐╨╡ ╤Д╨╕╨╗╤М╤В╤А╨░╤Ж╨╕╨╕ ╨│╨╛╤В╨╛╨▓╨╛╨│╨╛ ╤В╨╡╨║╤Б╤В╨░ (`enforce_constraints`).
-*   **[companion/reasoning.py](file:///C:/Games/companion/reasoning.py)**: ╨Ф╨▓╨╕╨╢╨╛╨║ ╤А╨░╤Б╤Б╤Г╨╢╨┤╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╣. ╨Ю╨┐╨╕╤Б╤Л╨▓╨░╨╡╤В ╨║╨╗╨░╤Б╤Б╤Л `Goal` (╨┤╨╛╨╗╨│╨╛╤Б╤А╨╛╤З╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╤Ж╨╡╨╗╨╕), `CausalLink` (╨┐╤А╨╕╤З╨╕╨╜╨╜╨╛-╤Б╨╗╨╡╨┤╤Б╤В╨▓╨╡╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╤Б╨▓╤П╨╖╨╕) ╨╕ `Prediction` (╨┐╤А╨╛╨│╨╜╨╛╨╖╤Л). ╨Ю╤В╨▓╨╡╤З╨░╨╡╤В ╨╖╨░ ╨░╨▓╤В╨╛╨╝╨░╤В╨╕╤З╨╡╤Б╨║╨╛╨╡ ╨▓╤Л╨┤╨╡╨╗╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╤Ж╨╡╨╗╨╡╨╣ ╨╕╨╖ ╤В╨╡╨║╤Б╤В╨░ ╨Ш╨▓╨░╨╜╨░ ╨╕ ╨┐╨╛╤Б╤В╤А╨╛╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╨║╨╛╨╜╤В╨╡╨║╤Б╤В╨░ ╤А╨░╤Б╤Б╤Г╨╢╨┤╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╣ ╨╜╨░ ╨│╨╛╤А╤П╤З╨╡╨╝ ╨┐╤Г╤В╨╕.

             logger.info("Retrying in %ds...", delay)
             await asyncio.sleep(delay)
     raise last_exc or RuntimeError("LLM call failed: unknown error")
+
+
+# тФАтФА Structured Outputs with Pydantic тФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФАтФА
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
+from typing import Literal, Dict, List, Optional
+
+class UserMood(BaseModel):
+    anxiety: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
+    anger: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
+    sadness: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
+    energy: float = Field(default=0.5, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
+
+class MessageAnalysis(BaseModel):
+    intent: Literal["world", "memory", "mixed", "command", "chat_casual"]
+    confidence: float = Field(default=0.5, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
+    user_mood: UserMood
+    user_state: Literal["ANXIOUS", "DEPRESSED", "CURIOUS", "OVERWHELMED", "NORMAL"]
+    estimated_importance: int = Field(default=5, ge=1, le=10)
+    command: str = Field(default="")
+
+class FactItem(BaseModel):
+    fact: str
+    memory_kind: Literal["permanent", "state", "event"]
+    importance: int = Field(default=5, ge=1, le=10)
+    confidence: float = Field(default=0.75, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
+    tags: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    evidence_messages: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+
+class FactExtractionResult(BaseModel):
+    facts: List[FactItem]
+
+class ConsolidationItem(BaseModel):
+    new_fact_index: int
+    existing_fact_id: str
+    relation: Literal["supersedes", "contradicts", "confirms", "related_to"]
+    reason: str = Field(default="")
+
+class ConsolidationResult(BaseModel):
+    relations: List[ConsolidationItem]
+
+class CausalLinkItem(BaseModel):
+    cause: str
+    effect: str
+    confidence: float = Field(default=0.75, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
+    evidence: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    mechanism: str = Field(default="")
+
+class CausalLinkExtractionResult(BaseModel):
+    links: List[CausalLinkItem]
+
+class ReflectionItem(BaseModel):
+    insight: str
+    importance: int = Field(default=7, ge=1, le=10)
+    confidence: float = Field(default=0.75, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
+
+class ReflectionResult(BaseModel):
+    reflections: List[ReflectionItem]
+
+class PersonalityPipelineResult(BaseModel):
+    interests_delta: Dict[str, int] = Field(default_factory=dict)
+    values_to_add: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    values_to_remove: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    fears_to_add: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    fears_to_remove: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    beliefs_to_add: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    beliefs_to_remove: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    motivation_to_add: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    motivation_to_remove: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    strengths_to_add: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    strengths_to_remove: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    weaknesses_to_add: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    weaknesses_to_remove: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    habits_delta: Dict[str, str] = Field(default_factory=dict)
+    relationships_delta: Dict[str, str] = Field(default_factory=dict)
+    addictions_delta: Dict[str, str] = Field(default_factory=dict)
+    changes: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+
+class KnowledgeDomainItem(BaseModel):
+    domain: str
+    confidence: float = Field(default=0.8, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
+
+class KnowledgeDomainsExtractionResult(BaseModel):
+    domains: List[KnowledgeDomainItem]
+
+
+def oneshot_structured(prompt: str, response_schema: type[BaseModel], model: str = MODEL_NAME) -> Any:
+    """Run generate_content with structured JSON output and exponential backoff retries."""
+    import time
+    import json
+    from companion.config import LLM_RETRIES, LLM_RETRY_DELAY
+    last_exc = None
+    config = make_config(
+        response_mime_type="application/json",
+        response_schema=response_schema,
+        temperature=0.1,
+    )
+    for attempt in range(LLM_RETRIES):
+        try:
+            r = client.models.generate_content(
+                model=model,
+                contents=prompt,
+                config=config,
+            )
+            text = (r.text or "").strip()
+            if not text:
+                raise ValueError("Empty response from model")
+            try:
+                data = json.loads(text)
+                return response_schema.model_validate(data)
+            except json.JSONDecodeError as jde:
+                logger.error("JSON decode failed on attempt %d: %s. Raw text: %r", attempt + 1, jde, text)
+                raise
+            except Exception as ve:
+                logger.error("Pydantic validation failed on attempt %d: %s. Raw text: %r", attempt + 1, ve, text)
+                raise
+        except Exception as e:
+            logger.error("oneshot_structured call failed (attempt %d/%d): %s", attempt + 1, LLM_RETRIES, e)
+            last_exc = e
+            if attempt < LLM_RETRIES - 1:
+                delay = LLM_RETRY_DELAY * (2 ** attempt)
+                time.sleep(delay)
+    raise last_exc or RuntimeError("oneshot_structured call failed: unknown error")
+
+
+async def oneshot_structured_async(prompt: str, response_schema: type[BaseModel], model: str = MODEL_NAME) -> Any:
+    """Async wrapper: runs the blocking structured call in a worker thread."""
+    return await asyncio.to_thread(oneshot_structured, prompt, response_schema, model)
+

+def _validate_master_summary(text: str | None) -> bool:
+    """Validate that master summary contains expected structure."""
+    if not text or len(text) < 100:
+        return False
+    expected_sections = ["[╨Ы╨╕╤З╨╜╨╛╤Б╤В╤М]", "[╨Ш╤Б╤В╨╛╤А╨╕╤П]", "[╨Ю╤В╨╜╨╛╤И╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П]", "[╨Я╨░╤В╤В╨╡╤А╨╜╤Л]", "[╨Р╨║╤В╤Г╨░╨╗╤М╨╜╨╛╨╡"]
+    matches = sum(1 for section in expected_sections if section in text)
+    return matches >= 2

+    # Migrate timeline events to SQLite if needed
+    from companion.config import TIMELINE_PATH
+    if os.path.exists(TIMELINE_PATH):
+        logger.info("Migrating timeline events from JSONL to SQLite...")
+        try:
+            import json
+            import hashlib
+            events = []
+            with open(TIMELINE_PATH, encoding="utf-8") as f:
+                for line in f:
+                    if not line.strip():
+                        continue
+                    try:
+                        e = json.loads(line)
+                        events.append(e)
+                    except Exception:
+                        pass
+            if events:
+                for e in events:
+                    h = hashlib.md5(f"{e['date']}_{e['event']}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:12]
+                    id_ = f"evt_{h}"
+                    memory_store.db.save_event(id_, e["date"], e["event"], e.get("importance", 5), e.get("description", ""))
+            bak_path = TIMELINE_PATH + ".bak"
+            os.rename(TIMELINE_PATH, bak_path)
+            logger.info("Timeline migration complete. Archived to %s", bak_path)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            logger.error("Timeline migration failed: %s", exc)
+
     try:

         logger.info("Vector index initialized: %s", result)
     except Exception as exc:
         logger.warning("Vector index initialization skipped: %s", exc)

+    if not memory_store.vector.test_embeddings():
+        logger.critical("Embedding API test failed on startup. Disabling vector retrieval.")
+        memory_store.vector.embeddings_enabled = False
+    else:
+        logger.info("Embedding API test successful.")

+        while len(bundle.to_prompt_block()) > self.char_budget:
+            if bundle.summaries:
+                bundle.summaries.pop()
+            elif bundle.reflections or bundle.causal_links:
+                if bundle.causal_links: bundle.causal_links.pop()
+                elif bundle.reflections: bundle.reflections.pop()
+            elif any(f.id not in pinned_ids for f in bundle.facts):
+                for i in range(len(bundle.facts) - 1, -1, -1):
+                    if bundle.facts[i].id not in pinned_ids:
+                        bundle.facts.pop(i)
+                        break
+            else:
+                # If only pinned facts remain in T3, we stop evicting facts and accept overflow (or break)
+                break
 
         return bundle

+
+    @property
+    def lock(self) -> asyncio.Lock:
+        """Lock for critical sections reading and mutating state.
+        
+        Contract:
+        - Ownership: The lock protects read-modify-write operations on shared files 
+          (e.g., personality.json) and multi-step dependent DB transactions.
+        - Acquisition: The CALLER (e.g., pipeline, background task) MUST acquire this 
+          lock BEFORE invoking methods that perform read-modify-write cycles. 
+          MemoryStore does NOT acquire this lock internally to avoid deadlocks.
+        - Blocking I/O: Do NOT hold this lock during long blocking I/O (like LLM calls). 
+          Synchronous file I/O inside the lock should be delegated to threads.
+        """
+        import asyncio
+        if not hasattr(self, "_lock"):
+            self._lock = asyncio.Lock()
+        return self._lock
+
+    def _assert_locked(self) -> None:
+        """Debug assertion to ensure the lock was acquired by the caller."""
+        if hasattr(self, "_lock") and not self._lock.locked():
+            logger.warning("DEBUG ASSERTION FAILED: store.lock is not held during critical mutation!")
+

+    def analyze_retrieval_effectiveness(self) -> dict[str, int]:
+        """Analyze retrieval metrics and adjust fact importance based on usage patterns."""
+        adjusted = {"boosted": 0, "lowered": 0}
+        try:
+            with self.db._conn() as conn:
+                rows = conn.execute("SELECT id, importance, facts_sent_count, facts_used_count, tags, memory_kind FROM facts WHERE status='active'").fetchall()
+                for row in rows:
+                    fid = row["id"]
+                    imp = int(row["importance"])
+                    sent = int(row["facts_sent_count"])
+                    used = int(row["facts_used_count"])
+                    tags = str(row["tags"] or "").lower()
+                    kind = str(row["memory_kind"] or "").lower()
+                    
+                    if kind == "permanent" or any(t in tags for t in ["anchor", "core_identity", "pinned"]):
+                        continue
+                        
+                    if sent > 10 and used == 0:
+                        new_imp = max(3, imp - 1)
+                        if new_imp != imp:
+                            conn.execute("UPDATE facts SET importance=?, facts_sent_count=0 WHERE id=?", (new_imp, fid))
+                            logger.info("memory_feedback_loop_applied: %s lowered from %d to %d", fid, imp, new_imp)
+                            adjusted["lowered"] += 1
+                    elif sent > 5 and used > 3:
+                        new_imp = min(8, imp + 1)
+                        if new_imp != imp:
+                            conn.execute("UPDATE facts SET importance=? WHERE id=?", (new_imp, fid))
+                            logger.info("memory_feedback_loop_applied: %s boosted from %d to %d", fid, imp, new_imp)
+                            adjusted["boosted"] += 1
+        except Exception as e:
+            logger.error("Retrieval effectiveness analysis failed: %s", e)
+        return adjusted

+    def _load_index(self) -> None:
+        import faiss
+        import numpy as np
+        
+        with self.lock:
+            with self._conn() as conn:
+                rows = conn.execute(
+                    "SELECT content, embedding, content_type FROM embeddings WHERE content_type != 'query'"
+                ).fetchall()
+            
+            self.content_list = []
+            self.hash_list = []
+            self.content_type_list = []
+            arr_vecs = []
+            
+            for content, blob, content_type in rows:
+                arr_vecs.append(_blob_to_float_list(blob))
+                self.content_list.append(content)
+                self.hash_list.append(self._content_hash(content))
+                self.content_type_list.append(content_type)
+                
+            self.index = faiss.IndexHNSWFlat(_EMBEDDING_DIM, 32)
+            if arr_vecs:
+                arr = np.array(arr_vecs, dtype=np.float32)
+                faiss.normalize_L2(arr)
+                self.index.add(arr)
+            self._is_initialized = True

+  def increment_meta(self, key: str, amount: int = 1) -> int:
+    with self._conn() as conn:
+      row = conn.execute(
+        """
+        INSERT INTO meta(key, value) VALUES(?, ?)
+        ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = CAST(CAST(meta.value AS INTEGER) + ? AS TEXT)
+        RETURNING value
+        """,
+        (key, str(amount), amount),
+      ).fetchone()
+      conn.commit()

+  def increment_fact_usage(self, fact_id: str, used: bool = False) -> None:
+    with self._conn() as conn:
+      if used:
+        conn.execute("UPDATE facts SET facts_sent_count = facts_sent_count + 1, facts_used_count = facts_used_count + 1 WHERE id=?", (fact_id,))

+  def increment_fact_usage_batch(self, sent_ids: list[str], used_ids: list[str]) -> None:
+    if not sent_ids and not used_ids:
+      return
+    with self._conn() as conn:
+      if sent_ids:
+        sent_only = [i for i in sent_ids if i not in used_ids]
+        if sent_only:
+          conn.executemany("UPDATE facts SET facts_sent_count = facts_sent_count + 1 WHERE id=?", [(i,) for i in sent_only])
+      if used_ids:
+        conn.executemany("UPDATE facts SET facts_sent_count = facts_sent_count + 1, facts_used_count = facts_used_count + 1 WHERE id=?", [(i,) for i in used_ids])
+

+  def list_summaries(self, status: str | None = None, limit: int | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
+    with self._conn() as conn:

+      params = []

+      q += " ORDER BY created_at DESC"
+      if limit:
